## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a clear investor-update route: title, disclaimer, agenda, earnings summary, outlook, announcements, then reconciliation. The strongest pages (6, 12) have good hierarchy, with green used for key figures, and the quarterly charts on 7 to 11 highlight the current quarter in green against grey history. Weaknesses are consequential: the announcements section (14 to 23) uses very small body text, with slides 14 to 18 and 20 to 23 running in tiny type and slide 2 an unreadable wall of legal text. Chart labels are small, the slide 6 margin line labels collide with the bars, and the announcements add little connection to the financial story before the appendix.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the slide 12 outlook layout with green key numbers and grey assumptions, and the green-highlighted current quarter bar on slides 6 to 11.

**Most useful repair:** Enlarge body text on slides 7 to 11 and 14 to 23, and fix the margin line labels on slide 6 where they overlap the bars.

**What would change the score:** I would cut each announcement slide to two or three bullets with one stated takeaway, and give slides 7, 8 and 9 headlines that state the point rather than just the segment name.

_Confidence medium. 28 of 28 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck opens with a title slide and a long forward-looking statements disclaimer covering risks and non-GAAP measures, a standard framing for an investor presentation. It then moves through a short agenda into an earnings summary for Q2 FY26, with highlights, a financial summary table and quarterly charts for Data Center, Gaming, Professional Visualization, Automotive and cash flow. A Q3 FY26 outlook slide follows, setting out revenue, margin, operating expense, other income and tax guidance. A section on key announcements then covers partnerships and product news in Europe, healthcare, manufacturing, cloud, MLPerf results, open models, RTX PRO servers, robotics and Jetson Thor. The closing section reconciles non-GAAP figures to GAAP for the quarter, prior quarters and the outlook. Slides worth studying include the financial summary table pairing GAAP and non-GAAP columns with Y/Y and Q/Q changes, the one-number-per-chart quarterly bar series, the outlook slide with its explicit assumptions, and the reconciliation tables that tie each adjustment to a footnote.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides does NVIDIA's Q2 FY26 investor presentation have?**

The NVIDIA Q2 FY26 investor presentation has 28 slides. Slide 1 is the title slide, Investor Presentation Q2 FY26, dated September 2025.

**What revenue outlook does NVIDIA give for Q3 FY26?**

On slide 12, NVIDIA gives a Q3 FY26 revenue outlook of $54.0 billion, plus or minus 2%. The outlook does not assume any H20 shipments to China.

**What were NVIDIA's Data Center results in Q2 FY26?**

Slide 7 reports Data Center revenue of $41,096 million in Q2 FY26, shown as 56% Y/Y and 5% Q/Q. Slide 5 describes this as Data Center up 56% Y/Y to $41.1B.

**How is NVIDIA's investor presentation structured?**

Slide 3 lists the deck's main sections as Q2 FY26 Earnings Summary, Key Announcements This Quarter, and Reconciliation of Non-GAAP to GAAP Financial Measures. The deck opens with a forward-looking statements disclaimer on slide 2.

**Text**

Certain matters in this presentation including, but not limited to, statements as to: the impact of H20 export licensing requirements; expectations with respect to growth, performance and benefits of NVIDIA’s
products, services, and technologies, including Blackwell, and related trends and drivers; expectations with respect to supply and demand for NVIDIA’s products, services, and technologies, including Blackwell,
and related matters including inventory, production and distribution; our financial position; projected market growth and trends, market opportunity, demand, and growth drivers; our financial and business
outlook; our dividend program; expectations with respect to NVIDIA’s third party arrangements, including with its collaborators and partners; third parties adopting our products and technologies; expectations
with respect to technology developments, including Rubin, and related trends and drivers; expectations with respect to AI and related industries; and other statements that are not historical facts are forward-
looking statements within the meaning of Section 27A of the Securities Act of 1933, as amended, and Section 21E of the Securities Exchange Act of 1934, as amended, which are subject to the “safe harbor”
created by those sections based on management’s beliefs and assumptions and on information currently available to management and are subject to risks and uncertainties that could cause results to be
materially different than expectations.
Important factors that could cause actual results to differ materially include: global economic and political conditions; NVIDIA’s reliance on third parties to manufacture, assemble, package and test NVIDIA’s
products; the impact of technological development and competition; development of new products and technologies or enhancements to NVIDIA’s existing product and technologies; market acceptance of
NVIDIA’s products or NVIDIA’s partners’ products; design, manufacturing or software defects; changes in consumer preferences or demands; changes in industry standards and interfaces; unexpected loss of
performance of NVIDIA’s products or technologies when integrated into systems; and changes in applicable laws and regulations, as well as other factors detailed from time to time in the most recent reports
NVIDIA files with the Securities and Exchange Commission, or SEC, including, but not limited to, its annual report on Form 10-K and quarterly reports on Form 10-Q. Copies of reports filed with the SEC are
posted on the company’s website and are available from NVIDIA without charge. These forward-looking statements are not guarantees of future performance and speak only as of the date hereof, and, except as
required by law, NVIDIA disclaims any obligation to update these forward-looking statements to reflect future events or circumstances.
Many of the products and features described herein remain in various stages and will be offered on a when-and-if-available basis. The statements within are not intended to be, and should not be interpreted as
a commitment, promise, or legal obligation, and the development, release, and timing of any features or functionalities described for our products is subject to change and remains at the sole discretion of
NVIDIA. NVIDIA will have no liability for failure to deliver or delay in the delivery of any of the products, features or functions set forth herein.
NVIDIA uses certain non-GAAP measures in this presentation including non-GAAP gross margin, non-GAAP gross margin excluding H20 related releases, non-GAAP operating income, non-GAAP net income, and
non-GAAP diluted earnings per share. NVIDIA believes the presentation of its non-GAAP financial measures enhances investors' overall understanding of the company's historical financial performance. The
presentation of the company's non-GAAP financial measures is not meant to be considered in isolation or as a substitute for the company's financial results prepared in accordance with GAAP, and the
company's non-GAAP measures may be different from non-GAAP measures used by other companies. Further information relevant to the interpretation of non-GAAP financial measures, and reconciliations of
these non-GAAP financial measures to the most comparable GAAP measures, may be found in the slide titled “Reconciliation of Non-GAAP to GAAP Financial Measures”.

### Slide 4

**Text**

Q2 FY26 / Earnings Summary

**Layout**

Single full-bleed image slide with a left-aligned two-line title in the upper-left quadrant, at about 4.3% left margin and 25.8% from the top. A thin green vertical bar sits flush against the left edge, spanning the height of the title block. No footer, slide number or other text; the NVIDIA logo image sits in the bottom-right corner.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed photograph of abstract glossy green sculptural waves. Several curved, layered ribbon-like bands sweep from the lower-right corner up and to the left, shaped like nested arches with bright specular edges. The upper-left area is near-black with faint scattered star-like specks. Lighting is directional from the right, with lime-green highlights (about #76B900 to #8DC63F) on the front bands and deep forest-green (about #1F3A08) shadows toward the left. The image covers the whole slide with no border, crop-shape or frame. A dark overlay (solid black fill) lies beneath the photo, making the upper-left darker. A small raster image of the NVIDIA logo wordmark is placed in the bottom-right corner at about 93% from the left and 94.7% from the top, and is boxed by its own rectangle, not cropped.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 with a full-slide photograph of green sculptural waves over it. Near-black background (#000000) with a photographic green wave image covering the slide. Title text is white (#FFFFFF) in NVIDIASans Bold, a geometric humanist sans-serif, with normal letter-spacing and no italics or capitals; title font size is about 5.6% of slide height, with the two lines set tight (about 6% line pitch). Accent colour is NVIDIA green #76B900, used for the left bar. No body text. Shapes are square-cornered with no shadows or borders. Gradients are present only within the photograph.

### Slide 5

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Headline-style bullets lead with the result, such as record revenue above outlook, and support it with specific figures in short lines.

**Text**

Highlights
Record quarter exceeded our outlook in what continues to be a dynamic external environment
•
Total revenue up 56% Y/Y to $46.7B, above outlook of $45.0B +/- 2%
•
Data Center up 56% Y/Y to $41.1B
•
Gaming up 49% Y/Y to $4.3B
Record Data Center revenue driven by continued Blackwell ramp
•
NVIDIA’s Blackwell platform reached record levels with 17% sequential growth
•
Cloud service providers, neo clouds, enterprises and sovereigns are all contributing to growth
•
We’re at the beginning of an industrial revolution; see $3 to $4 trillion in AI infrastructure spend by the end of the decade
Record Gaming revenue due to Blackwell adoption by gamers, creatives, and AI enthusiasts
•
Strong sales continued and we increased supply availability
•
For AI enthusiasts, on-device AI run best on RTX GPUs
•
With the RTX platform stack, Windows developers can create AI applications designed to run on the world's largest AI PC user base

**Layout**

Single-column text block on a black slide with a centred title near the top; three left-aligned sections, each a green heading followed by white bullet lines, start at about 6% from the left edge and run down to about 72% of the height. The NVIDIA logo sits bottom right and a thin green bar sits on the right edge at the bottom; no slide number is shown.

**Visuals**

Non-text elements: an NVIDIA logo (raster image, 1863 by 641 pixels) in the bottom-right corner, showing the green eye-shaped mark and the white-green NVIDIA wordmark on black; and a solid green (#76B900) vertical bar on the far right edge at the bottom. No charts, photographs or diagrams. Bullets are green dots set as separate text.

Background: Solid black #000000. Black background (#000000) with white title (#FFFFFF), green (#76B900) section headings, and white (#FFFFFF) body bullets with green (#76B900) bullet dots. Typeface is NVIDIA Sans, a geometric sans-serif: Bold for the title, Medium for the green headings, Regular for the body. Title is about 4.4% of slide height, headings about 2.7%, body about 2.35%. Bullets are plain round dots, no shapes, shadows, gradients or corner radii.

### Slide 6

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A dense financial summary table places GAAP and non-GAAP columns side by side with Y/Y and Q/Q changes, paired with a margin trend chart.
- Review: Chart labels are small, the slide 6 margin line labels collide with the bars, and the announcements add little connection to the financial story before the appendix.
- Worth keeping: Keep the slide 12 outlook layout with green key numbers and grey assumptions, and the green-highlighted current quarter bar on slides 6 to 11.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge body text on slides 7 to 11 and 14 to 23, and fix the margin line labels on slide 6 where they overlap the bars.

**Text**

Q2 FY26 Financial Summary / Revenue($M) / Non-GAAP GM / $46,743 / $44,062 / $39,331 / $35,082 / $30,040 / 75.7% / 75.0% / 73.5% / 61.0% / 72.7% / 51,500 / 46,500 / 41,500 / 36,500 / 31,500 / 26,500 / 21,500 / 16,500 / 11,500 / 6,500 / 1,500 / 100.0% / 95.0% / 90.0% / 85.0% / 80.0% / 75.0% / 70.0% / 65.0% / 60.0% / 55.0% / Q2 FY25 / Q3 FY25 / Q4 FY25 / Q1 FY26 / Q2 FY26 / GAAP / Non-GAAP / Q2 FY26 / Y/Y / Q/Q / Q2 FY26 / Y/Y / Q/Q / Revenue / $46,743 / +56% / +6% / $46,743 / +56% / +6% / Gross Margin / (GM) / 72.4% / -2.7 pts / +11.9 pts / 72.7% / -3.0 pts / +11.7 pts / GM excluding H20 / related charges/ / releases, net / 72.3% / +1.0 pt / Operating / Income / $28,440 / +53% / +31% / $30,165 / +51% / +30% / Net Income / $26,422 / +59% / +41% / $25,783 / +52% / +30% / Diluted EPS / $1.08 / +61% / +42% / $1.05 / +54% / +30% / Cash Flow / from Ops / $15,365 / +6% / -44% / $15,365 / +6% / -44% / All dollar figures are in millions other than EPS. Refer to Appendix for reconciliation of Non-GAAP measures.

**Layout**

Full-bleed black slide with a centred bold title across the top. A two-column arrangement sits below: a bordered combo chart on the left (about 4 to 49 percent of width, 23 to 82 percent of height) and a seven-column financial table on the right (about 51 to 96 percent of width, 24 to 90 percent of height), both top-aligned to the same band. The footnote is centred near the bottom edge at about 96 percent of height, and the NVIDIA logo sits bottom right with a thin green bar at the right edge. No slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Combo chart, left panel. Type: vertical bar chart with a line overlay on a secondary axis. Title: none printed on the chart. Bars: Revenue($M), five columns in order Q2 FY25 $30,040, Q3 FY25 $35,082, Q4 FY25 $39,331, Q1 FY26 $44,062, Q2 FY26 $46,743; the first four bars are grey #8C8C8C and the Q2 FY26 bar is green #76B900 and is highlighted. Left axis: revenue in millions of dollars, labels 1,500 to 51,500 in steps of 5,000 (1,500, 6,500, 11,500, 16,500, 21,500, 26,500, 31,500, 36,500, 41,500, 46,500, 51,500). Right axis: Non-GAAP gross margin in percent, labels 55.0% to 100.0% in steps of 5.0%. Line: Non-GAAP GM, white #FFFFFF stroke about 2 px, with point values Q2 FY25 75.7%, Q3 FY25 75.0%, Q4 FY25 73.5%, Q1 FY26 61.0% (lowest point, highlighted by its label at the bottom of the Q1 bar), Q2 FY26 72.7%. Category axis: Q2 FY25 to Q2 FY26 under a thin grey #8C8C8C baseline. Legend: centred above the plot, grey square swatch then Revenue($M), white line swatch then Non-GAAP GM. Chart frame: thin grey rectangular border around the whole chart. Table, right panel. Columns: row label, GAAP Q2 FY26, GAAP Y/Y, GAAP Q/Q, Non-GAAP Q2 FY26, Non-GAAP Y/Y, Non-GAAP Q/Q. Rows: Revenue $46,743 +56% +6% $46,743 +56% +6%; Gross Margin (GM) 72.4% -2.7 pts +11.9 pts 72.7% -3.0 pts +11.7 pts; GM excluding H20 related charges/ releases, net (italic) blank blank blank 72.3% blank +1.0 pt; Operating Income $28,440 +53% +31% $30,165 +51% +30%; Net Income $26,422 +59% +41% $25,783 +52% +30%; Diluted EPS $1.08 +61% +42% $1.05 +54% +30%; Cash Flow from Ops $15,365 +6% -44% $15,365 +6% -44%. NVIDIA logo: green eye mark with white NVIDIA wordmark, bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 across the whole slide. Black background with white bold titles and white table and chart text. Accent green #76B900 is used for the highlighted bar, the logo mark and the edge bar. Secondary text and the footnote are mid grey #8C8C8C. Table header cells use dark grey #464646 for the GAAP and Non-GAAP group row and #5E5E5E for the column row, with black body cells separated by thin grey #5E5E5E rules. Typeface: NVIDIA Sans, a geometric sans-serif; title in Bold, labels in Bold for row headers and Regular for figures, italic for the GM excluding H20 row. Title about 4.4 percent of slide height (cap height about 3.1 percent); body and table text about 1.7 percent; chart axis and bar labels about 1.5 percent; footnote about 1.4 percent. Lines are thin and square-cornered; no shadows or gradients.

**Layout**

Single slide with a centred bold title at the top, then two equal bordered panels side by side: a chart panel on the left (about 4.4% to 49.2% of the width) and a Highlights text panel on the right (about 50.8% to 95.6%), both spanning about 23.5% to 82.0% of the height. The chart caption 'Revenue ($M)' sits centred below the left panel. Side margins are about 4.4%, with the NVIDIA logo in the bottom-right corner; no page number or footer text is visible.

**Visuals**

Column chart of Data Center revenue in $M across five quarters, with no printed chart title inside the panel (the caption 'Revenue ($M)' sits below the panel). Categories along the horizontal axis: Q2 FY25, Q3 FY25, Q4 FY25, Q1 FY26, Q2 FY26. No vertical axis, tick marks or gridlines are printed; the bars sit on a thin horizontal baseline at about 75.2% of slide height, and bar heights are proportional to the labelled values, so the axis starts at zero (about). Single series with labelled values: Q2 FY25 $26,272; Q3 FY25 $30,771; Q4 FY25 $35,580; Q1 FY26 $39,112; Q2 FY26 $41,096. Legend: none. Highlighted point: the Q2 FY26 bar is filled green #76B900 while the other four are grey #8C8C8C. A green thin up-arrow above the Q2 FY26 bar carries the bold white annotation '56% Y/Y and 5% Q/Q'. Also a logo: the NVIDIA eye mark and wordmark in the bottom-right corner.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 across the full slide. Pure black background with white and NVIDIA-green accents. Title 'Data Center' in bold white (#FFFFFF), large, about 4.4% of slide height. Panel heading 'Highlights' in bold green #76B900. Body and chart labels in regular white #FFFFFF at about 2.2% of slide height; value and quarter labels at about 1.5%. Typeface is NVIDIA Sans, a sans-serif similar to a geometric grotesque, with Bold, Medium and Regular weights; bullets are small green dots in Arial. Bars are flat #8C8C8C with the highlight #76B900. Panels are thin 1px grey (about #5A5A5A) rectangles with square corners, no fill, no shadow, no gradient. Axis line is a thin grey line (#8C8C8C). Bottom-right has a short vertical green bar at the slide edge.

**Text**

Gaming
Revenue ($M)
$4,287 / 49% Y/Y and 14% Q/Q
$3,763
$3,279

$2,544

Record gaming revenue driven by the ramp of Blackwell GeForce GPUs, as strong sales continued and supply availability increased
Shipped GeForce RTX 5060 desktop GPUs this quarter

**Layout**

Single centred title at the top of a black slide, with two equal bordered panels side by side below it: a column chart on the left (x 4.4 to 49.2) and a Highlights bullet panel on the right (x 50.8 to 95.6), both spanning y 23.5 to 82.1 percent. The chart's axis title Revenue ($M) sits below the left panel, outside its border, left-aligned to the chart area. No footer text and no slide number; the NVIDIA logo sits bottom right at about x 93 to 99 percent, y 94.7 to 98.3 percent, and a thin green strip runs down the far right edge at the bottom.

**Visuals**

Column chart, titled by the axis label Revenue ($M), in the left panel. Five vertical bars on a single series, with values printed above each bar: Q2 FY25 $2,880; Q3 FY25 $3,279; Q4 FY25 $2,544; Q1 FY26 $3,763; Q2 FY26 $4,287. Categories run left to right along a horizontal baseline at about y 75.6 percent, labelled Q2 FY25, Q3 FY25, Q4 FY25, Q1 FY26, Q2 FY26. There is no printed y-axis, no tick marks and no printed range; bar heights are proportional to the printed values (the Q2 FY26 bar is about 1.5 times the height of the Q2 FY25 bar). Bars are mid-grey #8C8C8C except the latest quarter, Q2 FY26, which is highlighted in green #76B900. No legend. An annotation above the last bar reads 49% Y/Y and 14% Q/Q, with a thin green upward arrow (#76B900) to its left pointing up. Right panel contains a bulleted list with no chart. Logo: NVIDIA logo bottom right with green eye mark and white NVIDIA wordmark.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000. Pure black #000000 background with no gradient or texture. Title, data labels, body text and category labels in white #FFFFFF; accents in NVIDIA green #76B900 (Highlights heading, bullet dots, highlighted bar, annotation arrow, edge strip). Typeface is NVIDIA Sans, a humanist sans-serif: Bold for the title, Highlights heading and annotation; Regular for body text and labels; Medium for the axis title. Title cap height about 4.4 percent of slide height (font size 4.44 percent); body text font size 2.22 percent; chart labels 1.48 percent; axis title 2.47 percent. Panels are thin 1 px grey #5A5A5A outlines with square corners, no fill and no shadow. Bars have square corners, no outline, no shadow. Baseline is a thin grey #8C8C8C line. Bullets are green round dots set in Arial.

### Slide 9

**Review notes**

- Worth keeping: Keep the slide 12 outlook layout with green key numbers and grey assumptions, and the green-highlighted current quarter bar on slides 6 to 11.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge body text on slides 7 to 11 and 14 to 23, and fix the margin line labels on slide 6 where they overlap the bars.
- What would change the score: I would cut each announcement slide to two or three bullets with one stated takeaway, and give slides 7, 8 and 9 headlines that state the point rather than just the segment name.

**Text**

Professional Visualization / $601 / $511 / $509 / $486 / $454 / 32% Y/Y / and / 18% Q/Q / Q2 FY25 / Q3 FY25 / Q4 FY25 / Q1 FY26 / Q2 FY26 / Revenue ($M) / Highlights / • Growth driven by the adoption of high-end RTX workstation / GPUs for AI-powered workloads like design, simulation, and / prototyping / • Key customers are leveraging NVIDIA solutions to accelerate / their operations / • Activision Blizzard uses RTX workstations to enhance creative / workflows, while robotics innovator Figure AI powers its / humanoid robots with RTX embedded GPUs

**Layout**

Title centred in the top band at about 7% from the top. Two equal-width bordered panels sit side by side in the middle band, the chart panel on the left (x 4.4 to 49.2) and the Highlights panel on the right (x 50.8 to 95.6), both spanning y 23.5 to 82.1 with a gap of about 1.6 percent between them and margins of about 4.4 percent left and right. The chart's axis title Revenue ($M) sits centred under the left panel at y 83.7. The NVIDIA logo sits at bottom right, and there is no page number or footer text.

**Visuals**

Chart: vertical bar chart, revenue in $M. Five bars, no y-axis, no gridlines, no axis range printed; bar heights are proportional to the labelled values, with a shared baseline at y 74.9 percent. Series values printed above each bar: Q2 FY25 $454 (bar 26.2 percent of slide height, grey), Q3 FY25 $486 (28.0, grey), Q4 FY25 $511 (29.5, grey), Q1 FY26 $509 (29.3, grey), Q2 FY26 $601 (34.6, green). X-axis categories under the baseline: Q2 FY25, Q3 FY25, Q4 FY25, Q1 FY26, Q2 FY26. Highlighted point: the Q2 FY26 bar is green #76B900 and carries a thin green up-arrow with the annotation 32% Y/Y and 18% Q/Q above it. No legend. Other visuals: NVIDIA logo at bottom right and a green vertical strip at the far bottom-right edge.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 (a green #76B900 fill underlies it and is fully covered). Pure black background. Titles and body text white #FFFFFF; accent green #76B900 for the Highlights heading, bullet dots, the highlighted bar, the arrow and the edge strip; chart bars grey #8C8C8C. Typeface: NVIDIASans, a geometric grotesque sans-serif; Bold for the title, callout and Highlights heading, Regular for body and value labels, Medium for the axis title, Arial for the bullet glyphs. Title cap height about 4.4 percent of slide height (about 50 pt equivalent); body text about 2.2 percent (about 25 pt equivalent); data labels about 1.5 percent. Panels have a thin grey outline about #5C5C5C, square corners, no shadow. Bars are flat with square corners and no outline. No gradients, no shadows, no rounded corners.

Automotive / Highlights / • Growth primarily driven by self-driving solutions / • Began shipments of NVIDIA Thor SoC, the successor to Orin / • NVIDIA’s full-stack DRIVE AV software platform is now in / production, opening up billions in new revenue opportunities / for NVIDIA while improving vehicle safety and autonomy / 69% Y/Y / and / 3% Q/Q / $346 / $449 / $570 / $567 / $586 / Q2 FY25 / Q3 FY25 / Q4 FY25 / Q1 FY26 / Q2 FY26 / Revenue ($M)

**Layout**

Single slide with a centred title at the top and two equal bordered panels side by side below it: a bar chart panel on the left and a Highlights text panel on the right, both spanning roughly 23.5% to 82% of the slide height. The chart’s axis caption “Revenue ($M)” sits centred below the left panel. **Visuals**

Vertical bar chart titled by the caption Revenue ($M), with no y-axis, no gridlines and no printed axis scale; the horizontal baseline is a thin grey line at about 75% of slide height spanning the chart width. Annotation at top right of chart: a thin green upward arrow with the text 69% Y/Y and 3% Q/Q pointing up toward the highlighted Q2 FY26 bar. Single series, no legend. Right panel contains bullet text only, no image.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 background.

### Slide 11

**Review notes**

- Worth keeping: Keep the slide 12 outlook layout with green key numbers and grey assumptions, and the green-highlighted current quarter bar on slides 6 to 11.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge body text on slides 7 to 11 and 14 to 23, and fix the margin line labels on slide 6 where they overlap the bars.

**Text**

Sources & Uses of Cash / Highlights / • Y/Y increase reflects growth in revenue, partially offset by an / increase in working capital / • Q/Q decrease was mainly driven by $8.1B in taxes paid in the / quarter / • Returned $10.0B to shareholders in the second quarter through / $9.7B of share repurchases and $244M of cash dividends / • Invested $1.9B in capex (includes principal payments on PP&E) / • Ended the quarter with $56.8B in gross cash and $8.5B in debt; / $48.3B in net cash / Cash Flow from Operations ($M) / $14,489 / $17,629 / $16,628 / $27,414 / $15,365 / Q2 FY25 / Q3 FY25 / Q4 FY25 / Q1 FY26 / Q2 FY26 / 6% Y/Y / 44% Q/Q / Gross cash is defined as cash/cash equivalents & marketable securities. / Net cash is defined as gross cash less debt. / Debt is defined as principal value of debt.

**Layout**

Single-slide grid of a title band and two equal bordered panels side by side: a left chart panel and a right highlights panel, each spanning about 45% of the slide width and sharing the same top and bottom edges (about 23.5% to 82.1% of slide height). The title is centred horizontally at the top (about 7% from the top). The chart caption sits below the left panel and three italic definition lines sit bottom-left, left-aligned at about 4.5% from the left edge. The NVIDIA logo sits bottom-right, with a thin green bar at the far right edge. No slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Vertical bar chart titled Cash Flow from Operations ($M), with a horizontal baseline (about 6.7% to 46.5% of slide width, at about 75.2% of slide height). Five bars, values printed above each bar, no visible y-axis or gridlines: Q2 FY25 $14,489 (grey #8C8C8C, about height 21.3% of slide), Q3 FY25 $17,629 (grey, about 25.9%), Q4 FY25 $16,628 (grey, about 24.4%), Q1 FY26 $27,414 (grey, about 40.2%), Q2 FY26 $15,365 (green #76B900, about 22.5%, highlighted as the current quarter). Bar heights are proportional to the printed values. Category labels under the baseline in white. No legend. Annotation to the right of the last bar: a small green upward arrow (about x 40.2%, y 40.0% to 43.1%) with the label 6% Y/Y in bold white, and a small red downward arrow (about x 40.2%, y 44.8% to 48.0%) with 44% Q/Q in bold white. Right panel contains no graphics other than the bullets.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 across the whole slide. Black background (#000000). Title in white #FFFFFF, bold geometric sans-serif (NVIDIA Sans Bold), about 4.4% of slide height. Section heading Highlights in bold NVIDIA green #76B900 at about 2.2%. Body text in regular NVIDIA Sans, white #FFFFFF, about 2.2% of slide height. Bullet dots in green #76B900 (Arial). Chart labels regular white at about 1.5%. Chart caption medium weight white at about 2.5%. Footnotes italic grey #8C8C8C at about 1.5%. Panels are thin 1px-style rectangles with square corners and a grey outline about #595959, no fill, no shadows. Bars are flat rectangles with square corners. Accent green #76B900 used for the heading, bullets, highlighted bar and logo.

### Slide 12

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: The outlook slide states each guidance line with its assumptions, such as excluding H20 shipments, making the forecast easy to scan.
- Worth keeping: Keep the slide 12 outlook layout with green key numbers and grey assumptions, and the green-highlighted current quarter bar on slides 6 to 11.

**Text**

Q3 FY26 Outlook / Revenue / $54.0 billion, plus or minus 2% / Does not assume any H20 shipments to China / If geopolitical issues recede, could ship $2 to $5 billion dollars in H20 revenue in Q3 / Gross Margins / 73.3% GAAP and 73.5% non-GAAP, plus or minus 50 basis points / Continue to expect to exit the year with non-GAAP gross margins in the mid-70% range / Operating Expense / Approximately $5.9 billion GAAP and $4.2 billion non-GAAP / Expect full year fiscal 2026 operating expense growth to be in the high-30% range / Other Income & Expense / Income of approximately $500 million for GAAP and non-GAAP / Excluding gains and losses from non-marketable and publicly-held equity securities / Tax Rate / 16.5% GAAP and non-GAAP, plus or minus 1%, excluding discrete items / Refer to Appendix for reconciliation of Non-GAAP measures.

**Layout**

A single centred title sits at the top of the slide, above a full-width five-row table with a thin rule above and below each row. Each row has a left label column (about 14.8% to 34% of the width) separated by a thin vertical rule from a right value column starting at about 35.7%. Row labels are left-aligned at x 14.8%; values are left-aligned at x 35.7%. The table spans x 13.75% to 86.25%. A centred footnote sits below the table at y 84.8%. The NVIDIA logo sits bottom right at x 93%, y 94.7%; a thin green bar sits at the bottom-right edge. No slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

None apart from the table structure: a five-row, two-column table with no cell fills. Column 1 (labels): Revenue; Gross Margins; Operating Expense; Other Income & Expense; Tax Rate. Column 2 (values): Revenue row: $54.0 billion, plus or minus 2%; sub-lines 'Does not assume any H20 shipments to China' and 'If geopolitical issues recede, could ship $2 to $5 billion dollars in H20 revenue in Q3'. Gross Margins row: 73.3% GAAP and 73.5% non-GAAP, plus or minus 50 basis points; sub-line 'Continue to expect to exit the year with non-GAAP gross margins in the mid-70% range'. Operating Expense row: Approximately $5.9 billion GAAP and $4.2 billion non-GAAP; sub-line 'Expect full year fiscal 2026 operating expense growth to be in the high-30% range'. Other Income & Expense row: Income of approximately $500 million for GAAP and non-GAAP; sub-line 'Excluding gains and losses from non-marketable and publicly-held equity securities'. Tax Rate row: 16.5% GAAP and non-GAAP, plus or minus 1%, excluding discrete items. Key figures are set in bold green #76B900. The NVIDIA logo: green eye-shaped mark with white 'NVIDIA' wordmark, bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000. Pure black background (#000000) with white and green accents and grey secondary text. Titles and row labels are white #FFFFFF; body values are white #FFFFFF; key figures are green #76B900 in bold; secondary sub-lines are grey #8C8C8C. Typeface is NVIDIA Sans (a geometric humanist sans-serif): Bold for the title and highlighted figures, Medium for row labels, Regular for values and notes. Title is about 4.4% of slide height; row labels about 2.7%; values about 2.5%; sub-lines about 1.7%; footnote about 1.4%. Rules are thin mid-grey lines (about 0.1% of height) in #8C8C8C. No shapes with corner radii, shadows or gradients.

### Slide 13

**Text**

Key Announcements
This Quarter

**Layout**

Full-bleed dark slide with a left-aligned two-line title in the upper-left third, starting at about 4.3% from the left edge and sitting at about 26% to 37% of the slide height. A thin green bar runs down the left edge from the top, about 36% of the height. No footer, page number or other furniture is visible.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed abstract 3D render covering the whole slide: glossy green curved ribbons or layers sweep diagonally from the lower-left toward the upper-right, with the largest, brightest forms in the lower-right and the layers thinning and darkening toward the upper-left. The upper-left area is near-black with faint specks and small pale blue-white points of light, resembling distant stars. Lighting is directional, with bright green highlights and thin pale rim lines along the ribbon edges. Dominant colours are black #000000 and greens from about #2E5A00 to #76B900. The image is not cropped, boxed or tinted; it runs edge to edge. A small image sits at the bottom right (about x 93%, y 94.7%, 5.9% by 3.6%), probably a logo, but it is not distinguishable against the green ribbon.

**Design**

Background is solid black #000000 with the full-bleed render on top. Title is white #FFFFFF in NVIDIASans Bold, a geometric-humanist sans-serif, at about 5.56% of slide height, set on two lines with tight leading. Accent is a vertical bar in NVIDIA green #76B900 at the left edge (1.5% wide, 36.4% tall). No shapes, shadows, corner radii or gradients beyond the photographic lighting of the render.

### Slide 14

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are consequential: the announcements section (14 to 23) uses very small body text, with slides 14 to 18 and 20 to 23 running in tiny type and slide 2 an unreadable wall of legal text.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge body text on slides 7 to 11 and 14 to 23, and fix the margin line labels on slide 6 where they overlap the bars.

**Text**

Europe Builds Over 3,000 / Exaflops of NVIDIA AI / Infrastructure to Fuel Region’s / Next Industrial Transformation / EU plans to invest 20B Euros to / establish 20 AI factories including 5 / "gigafactories," to increase its AI / compute infrastructure 10x / • NVIDIA is working with European nations, and technology and / industry leaders, to build NVIDIA Blackwell AI infrastructure that / will strengthen digital sovereignty, support economic growth and / position the continent as a leader in the AI industrial revolution / • In France, Mistral AI is building an end-to-end cloud platform / powered by 18K NVIDIA Grace Blackwell systems in the first / phase, with plans to expand across multiple sites in 2026 / • In the U.K., NVIDIA Cloud Partners Nebius and Nscale announced / the first phase of their AI infrastructure development plans to / deploy 14K Blackwell GPUs to power new data centers / • In Germany, NVIDIA and partners are building the world’s first / industrial AI cloud for European manufacturers, powered by DGX / B200 systems and RTX PRO Servers / • These deployments will enable the EU to securely develop, train / and deploy agentic and physical AI applications / 3X+ / 2024 / 2025 / 2026 / GPU Die

**Layout**

Two-column split slide on a black field. The left column (about 0 to 47% of width) holds a four-line bold title at top left, a thin horizontal rule beneath it at about 27.6% of height, and a five-item bullet list below. The right column (about 55 to 100% of width) is a darker charcoal panel holding a Europe map with a callout, three bars for 2024 to 2026 and their labels. Title and bullets share a left edge at about 7.3% of width. The NVIDIA logo sits bottom left at about 1% from the left edge and 95% from the top. No slide number or footer text is visible.

**Visuals**

A bar chart with a Europe map as backdrop. Chart type: three vertical bars on a horizontal baseline, with an optional caption-style title 'GPU Die' under the year labels. X axis: years 2024, 2025, 2026. Y axis: no axis, scale or units are printed; bar heights are relative only. Series: one series in green gradient bars. Values are not printed; relative heights are about 1.0 (2024), about 2.7 (2025) and about 5.9 (2026), so 2025 is about 3X the 2024 bar as annotated by '3X+' and 2026 about 2.2X the 2025 bar. Callout arrows: 2025 bar has an upward beige arrow from the top of the bar to a short beige horizontal cap line labelled 3X+ above it; 2026 bar has a tall upward beige arrow with a short cap line at its top. Legend: none. Highlighted points: the 2026 bar is the tallest. Map: a dark bronze-brown 3D rendering of Europe (UK, Ireland, Iberia, France, Germany, Italy, Scandinavia, Eastern Europe) with small dark grey 3D map pins scattered across it and faint country borders, lit from the upper left, slightly tinted and blended into the charcoal panel, cropped at the right and bottom edges of the slide. Bars: lime green #B5FF2E at top fading to #5E9E00 at bottom with flat tops. Baseline: thin grey line #8C8C8C from about 72% to 94% of width at 78% of height. Arrows and cap lines: beige #C1B485, about 1 px stroke, with arrowheads.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 across the full slide, with a charcoal #151617 panel at 85% opacity over the right 45% of the slide. Black background (#000000) with a charcoal panel (#151617 at 85% opacity) covering the right column, which has a faint vertical gradient edge at its left side. Title: white #FFFFFF, NVIDIA Sans Bold (a geometric grotesque sans-serif), tight leading of about 5.4% of height per line, title cap height about 3.1% of height, so text size about 4.4% of height. Body: white #FFFFFF NVIDIA Sans Regular, size about 2.2% of height, line pitch about 2.4% of height, bullets in green #76B900 with extra space between items. Callout text: white NVIDIA Sans Regular, size about 2.5% of height, centred. Accent green #76B900 for bullets and bars; beige #C1B485 for '3X+' and arrows (NVIDIA Sans Medium). Rule under title: thin light grey line about 1 px. Shapes are square-cornered with no shadows.

### Slide 15

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are consequential: the announcements section (14 to 23) uses very small body text, with slides 14 to 18 and 20 to 23 running in tiny type and slide 2 an unreadable wall of legal text.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge body text on slides 7 to 11 and 14 to 23, and fix the margin line labels on slide 6 where they overlap the bars.

**Text**

NVIDIA Partners With Europe
Model Builders and Cloud Providers
to Accelerate Region’s Leap Into AI
•
NVIDIA is teaming with model builders and cloud providers across
Europe and the Middle East to optimize sovereign LLMs, helping
to accelerate enterprise AI adoption for the region’s industries
•
Model builders and AI consortiums are using NVIDIA Nemotron,
to maximize model cost efficiency and accuracy for enterprise AI
workloads, including agentic AI
•
Model post-training and inference will run on AI infrastructure in
Europe from NVIDIA Cloud Partners with DGX Cloud Lepton
•
The open, sovereign models will provide a foundation for an
integrated regional AI ecosystem that reflects local languages
and culture
NVIDIA

**Layout**

Two-zone layout: a black text column on the left (about 0 to 55% of width) and a dark 45% panel on the right (x 55.1 to 100) carrying a Europe map with a logo grid. The three-line title sits top-left at x 7.3%, y 7.7%, left-aligned with a 7.3% left margin. A thin grey horizontal divider sits at y 27.6% running from x 0 to about 47%. Four bullets start at y 34.2% at x 7.3% (bullet dots) and text at x 8.6%. The NVIDIA logo sits bottom-left at x 0.8%, y 94.7%. A small green bar sits at the bottom-left edge. There is no slide number, date, or footer text.

**Visuals**

One large visual: a dark, embossed, bronze-brown relief map of Europe (about 55.1% to 100% of width, full height) with country borders and small raised pin markers, partly covered by a #151617 overlay at 85% opacity and a dark gradient fading in from the left edge of the panel. Over the map sit 19 white company logos arranged in a loose 4-column by 5-row grid, listed in the elements. The map is lit from above and fades into black at the right and bottom. No charts, tables, or photographs. The NVIDIA logo bottom-left (green eye mark with white wordmark) is the only other graphic.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 with a dark #151617 (85% opacity) map panel covering the right 45% of the slide. Black background (#000000) with a dark #151617 map panel at 85% opacity on the right. Title in white #FFFFFF, NVIDIA Sans Bold (a geometric-humanist sans-serif, bold weight), about 4.4% of slide height per line, left-aligned, line spacing about 4.8% of height. Body text in white #FFFFFF, NVIDIA Sans Regular, about 2.2% of slide height, line spacing about 2.4% of height, paragraph gap about 1.4%. Bullet dots and accent in NVIDIA green #76B900. Divider is a thin light grey (#8C8C8C) line about 0.2% of height. Logos in white. No rounded corners, shadows, or gradients on text; the map panel has a soft left-edge gradient from black.

### Slide 16

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are consequential: the announcements section (14 to 23) uses very small body text, with slides 14 to 18 and 20 to 23 running in tiny type and slide 2 an unreadable wall of legal text.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge body text on slides 7 to 11 and 14 to 23, and fix the margin line labels on slide 6 where they overlap the bars.

**Text**

NVIDIA Partners With Novo
Nordisk and DCAI to Advance
Drug Discovery
NVIDIA and Novo Nordisk are collaborating to accelerate drug
discovery through innovative AI use cases
Novo Nordisk will use the Gefion sovereign AI supercomputer –
powered by NVIDIA DGX SuperPOD – for running drug discovery
and agentic AI workloads
And also:
NVIDIA BioNeMo for generative AI-powered drug discovery
NVIDIA NIM and NeMo for building customized agentic workflows
NVIDIA Omniverse to create physically accurate simulation
environments for developing physical AI applications
Will use NVIDIA BioNeMo, NIM, and NeMo to create customized
generative AI models and AI agents that can be used for early
research and clinical development
NVIDIA Omniverse will be used to apply advanced simulation and
physical AI technologies
GEFION
Specifications
Powered by NVIDIA DGX Technology

**Layout**

Two-column split: a black text column on the left (about 0 to 55% of width) and a full-height photograph filling the right column from x 55.1% to the right edge. The title sits top-left at x 7.3%, y 7.7%, left-aligned, in three lines. A thin grey horizontal rule runs across the text column at about y 27.6% from the left margin to about x 47%. The bulleted body starts at x 7.3% (bullet) and x 8.6% (text) below the rule, left-aligned with a ragged right edge. The NVIDIA logo sits in the bottom-left corner at x 0.8%, y 94.7%, with a small green bar at the far left edge beside it. No slide number and no footer text other than the logo and the DCAI caption on the photo.

**Visuals**

One photograph in the right column, from x 55.1% to 100% and full height (0 to 100%), full-bleed to the top, bottom and right edges, with a dark tint over it. Subject: an indoor AI supercomputer installation. Overhead, a yellow cable tray or cooling duct runs diagonally across the top of the frame with cables and yellow fixtures hanging from it. In the middle left, two tall black server cabinets with glass doors sit side by side; the left door shows a small NVIDIA logo near its base and the right door shows the word EVIDEN near its base; the glass shows grey shelving with server units. In the foreground right, a tall black cabinet with a large white bold sans-serif GEFION wordmark at its top carries an orange rectangular panel with a black silhouette of a charging bull, head and horns raised, seen from the front. Below the panel, white small text reads Specifications / Powered by NVIDIA DGX Technology / 191 NVIDIA DGX H100 / 1,528 NVIDIA H100 Tensor Core GPUs. Composition: a three-quarter angled view from the left, camera at about shoulder height, the cabinets filling the frame and cropped at the top and right edge. Lighting: bright, even indoor lighting with soft reflections on the glass; the floor is pale grey-white. Dominant colours: saffron yellow at the top, black cabinets, orange panel, pale grey-white floor and walls. The photo is cropped by the slide edges, not boxed. A dark tint (#151617 at 85% opacity) covers the photo region, slightly dimming it. The DCAI caption sits on the lower left of the photo in dark text. A placed 1863 by 641 pixel image sits at x 93.0%, y 94.7%, and a logo at bottom-left x 0.8%, y 94.7%; the bottom-right image is not discernible in the render. Logo: the NVIDIA eye mark with NVIDIA wordmark in white at the bottom-left corner.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 across the full slide, with the photograph covering the right column. Dark, high-contrast black slide. Background black #000000 across the whole slide. Body text in white #FFFFFF, NVIDIASans Regular (sans-serif), about 2.2% of slide height for main bullets and about 2.0% for sub-bullets, with generous line spacing (about 2.4% between bullets). **Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are consequential: the announcements section (14 to 23) uses very small body text, with slides 14 to 18 and 20 to 23 running in tiny type and slide 2 an unreadable wall of legal text.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge body text on slides 7 to 11 and 14 to 23, and fix the margin line labels on slide 6 where they overlap the bars.

**Text**

Siemens and NVIDIA Expand Partnership to Accelerate AI Capabilities in Manufacturing
Siemens and NVIDIA announced an expansion of their partnership to accelerate the next era of industrial AI and digitalization and enable the factory of the future
The partnership empowers industrial companies to leverage AI-powered technologies for next-gen factory automation
Advanced AI agents will work seamlessly across the Siemens Industrial Copilot portfolio, executing entire AI-powered processes without human intervention
Siemens’ Operations Copilot brings generative AI to shopfloor operators; optimized to run on premises with NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell Server Edition GPUs
Siemens is also integrating NVIDIA BlueField DPUs for AI-driven cybersecurity
BMW GROUP
Product Design and Engineering
Secure Edge Industrial AI
SCHAEFFLER
Operational Planning and Optimization
foxconn
Comprehensive Product and Factory Digital Twin
Production Optimization
SIEMENS
NVIDIA

**Layout**

Two-column split: the left column (about 0 to 45% of width) holds a three-line bold title at top left at about 7.3% from the left edge, a thin grey horizontal rule below it at about 27.6% of height, and five bulleted points left-aligned from about 8.6% of width. The right column (about 55% to 100% of width) is a darker panel holding the Siemens and NVIDIA logo lockup at top and a diagram below, with a faint vertical gradient fade at its left edge. The NVIDIA logo sits bottom-left at about 0.8% from the left edge and 94.7% from the top; no slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

One isometric digital-twin diagram in the right column, about 55.1% to 100% of width and 38.6% to 84.4% of height, rendered on black with thin white and grey line paths. It shows a factory floor plan with a glowing teal-lit building, conveyor-like route lines and dotted paths connecting five labelled photo cards: (1) BMW GROUP label tag above a photo of a light silver-blue car in a design studio, captioned Product Design and Engineering, placed upper left; (2) SCHAEFFLER label tag above a photo of an industrial workstation with green-lit robots and workers, captioned Operational Planning and Optimization, placed lower left; (3) foxconn label tag above a photo of a factory production line with a worker, captioned Comprehensive Product and Factory Digital Twin, placed bottom centre; (4) a photo of server hardware (GPU cards and stacked rack units) captioned Secure Edge Industrial AI, placed upper right; (5) a photo of a worker at a monitor on a production line, captioned Production Optimization, placed lower right. Thin grey connector lines link the cards to the factory. Two logos: the Siemens wordmark in teal and the NVIDIA logo in green and white. Photos are small rectangular cards with thin light-grey borders, dark and moody lighting with cool blue-white and green accents.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000, with a slightly lighter #010101 panel covering the right column. Black background (#000000) throughout, with a slightly lighter right panel (#010101) fading in from the left edge. Title text white #FFFFFF in NVIDIASans Bold, about 4.4% of slide height per line, with three lines and tight leading. Body text white #FFFFFF in NVIDIASans Regular, about 2.2% of slide height, with about 2.4% line spacing. Bullet glyphs and accent colour are NVIDIA green #76B900. Rule and divider lines are light grey #8C8C8C, about 1 px. Siemens wordmark teal #009999. No shadows, gradients only as the faint panel fade, and no rounded corners on the card photos (square corners with thin borders).

### Slide 18

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are consequential: the announcements section (14 to 23) uses very small body text, with slides 14 to 18 and 20 to 23 running in tiny type and slide 2 an unreadable wall of legal text.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge body text on slides 7 to 11 and 14 to 23, and fix the margin line labels on slide 6 where they overlap the bars.

**Text**

NVIDIA DGX Cloud Lepton / Connects Europe’s Developers to / Global NVIDIA Compute Ecosystem / NVIDIA is expanding NVIDIA DGX Cloud Lepton — an AI platform / featuring a global compute marketplace that connects / developers building agentic and physical AI applications — with / GPUs now available from a growing network of cloud providers / Mistral AI, Nebius, Nscale, Firebird, Fluidstack, Hydra Host, / Scaleway and Together AI are now contributing NVIDIA Blackwell / and other NVIDIA architecture GPUs to the marketplace / AWS and Microsoft Azure will be the first large-scale cloud / providers to participate in DGX Cloud Lepton, joining CoreWeave, / Crusoe, Firmus, Foxconn, GMI Cloud, Lambda and Yotta Data / Services / Hugging Face is introducing Training Cluster as a Service, / seamlessly connecting AI researchers and developers building / foundation models with the NVIDIA compute ecosystem

**Layout**

Two-column split: a text column occupies the left 55 percent of the slide on black and a full-height photograph fills the right 45 percent. The title sits top-left at about 7.7 percent from the top, left-aligned at x 7.3 percent, and is followed by a thin horizontal rule at about 27.6 percent of the height spanning from the left margin to about 47 percent of the width. Four bullets are left-aligned starting at x 7.3 percent from about 34 percent to 72 percent of the height. The NVIDIA logo sits bottom-left at about 95 percent of the height inside a green edge bar. No page number is visible.

**Visuals**

One photograph, a dark, gold-tinted render of planet Earth seen from space, placed at x 55.1 percent, y 0, w 44.9 percent, h 100 percent (788 by 988 pixels), cropped by the slide edges at the right and bottom and running full height as a panel. The globe's limb curves across the upper-left of the panel; the sunlit continents of Europe, Africa and Asia glow pale gold on the upper-middle and left, while the lower half fades into near-black. Many thin glowing gold and white arcs, like flight paths or data links, radiate from the lower-left across the globe, with small bright points of light scattered over the surface, especially in the centre. Lighting is a single warm key light from upper left; the composition is off-centre with the planet filling most of the panel. A dark gradient strip (722 by 4500 pixels) covers the left 9 percent of the panel, fading from black at the left edge into the globe image. ### Slide 19

**Review notes**

- Suggested repair: Enlarge body text on slides 7 to 11 and 14 to 23, and fix the margin line labels on slide 6 where they overlap the bars.

**Text**

NVIDIA Blackwell Delivers / Breakthrough Performance in / Latest MLPerf Training Results / • NVIDIA AI platform delivered the highest performance at scale on / every benchmark and powered every result submitted on the / benchmark’s toughest large language model (LLM)-focused test: / Llama 3.1 405B pretraining – first MLPerf training submissions / using NVIDIA GB200 NVL72 systems / • NVIDIA was the only platform to submit results on every MLPerf / Training v5.0 benchmark – underscoring its exceptional / performance and versatility / • These performance leaps highlight advancements in Blackwell / that enable faster and more cost-effective training / • NVIDIA is working with companies worldwide to build out AI / factories – speeding the training and deployment of next-gen / agentic AI applications that produce tokens and intelligence that / can be applied to almost every industry and academic domain / NVIDIA

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a full-black slide. The left text column runs from about 7% to 47% of the slide width: the three-line title sits top-left at about 7.7% from the top, a thin horizontal divider sits at about 27.6% of the height spanning from the left edge to about 47% of the width, and the four-bullet list starts at about 34% and ends at about 73% of the height, all left aligned with a left margin of about 7.3%. The right panel from about 55% to 100% of the width holds the illustration, vertically placed between about 28% and 72% of the height. The NVIDIA logo sits bottom-left at about 1% from the left edge and about 95% from the top, with a thin green bar along the bottom-left corner. No slide number or footer text is visible.

**Visuals**

One isometric 3D illustration on the right: a rack-scale system of black server cabinets. A tall rear cabinet stands at the back and a long, lower cabinet row with lit green and cyan-blue module panels sits in front of it. At the left front, a small black cooling or power unit stands on the floor, and silver-grey coolant pipes loop across the base and run to the right. The render has no outline or box and blends into the black background. Lighting is a soft top key with thin specular edge highlights on the cabinet edges and pipes. Dominant colours are black, dark charcoal, lime green and cyan LED accents, and silver-grey pipes. The image is not cropped, is not full-bleed, and carries no overlay or tint. A vertical gradient strip, slightly lighter at its left edge and fading to black, lies along the left edge of the right panel. No charts, tables or photographs appear on the slide.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 across the whole slide. Black background with white titles and white body text, lime green #76B900 used for bullet dots and the logo and bottom-left accent bar. Light grey #A0A0A0 divider line. Typeface is NVIDIA Sans, a geometric grotesque sans-serif: Bold for the title and Regular for the body. The title is about 4.4% of slide height, about 4.8% line pitch, and the body is about 2.2% of slide height with about 2.4% line pitch and extra space between bullets. Flat design with no shadows, no rounded corners, and no gradients on the text panel; the only gradient is the subtle vertical strip at the right panel's left edge.

### Slide 20

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are consequential: the announcements section (14 to 23) uses very small body text, with slides 14 to 18 and 20 to 23 running in tiny type and slide 2 an unreadable wall of legal text.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge body text on slides 7 to 11 and 14 to 23, and fix the margin line labels on slide 6 where they overlap the bars.

**Text**

OpenAI and NVIDIA Propel AI
Innovation With New Open Models
Optimized for the World’s Largest
AI Inference Infrastructure
•  OpenAI’s two new open-weight AI reasoning models, oss-120b
and gpt-oss-20b, bring cutting-edge AI development to
developers, enthusiasts, enterprises, startups and governments
everywhere
•  This collaboration highlights the power of community-driven
innovation and NVIDIA’s foundational role in making AI accessible
worldwide
•  The models were trained on NVIDIA H100 GPUs and run inference
best on the hundreds of millions of GPUs running the NVIDIA
CUDA platform
•  With software optimizations, the models offer optimal inference
on NVIDIA GB200 NVL72 systems, achieving industry leading
1.5M tokens per second, driving massive efficiency for inference

### Slide 21

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are consequential: the announcements section (14 to 23) uses very small body text, with slides 14 to 18 and 20 to 23 running in tiny type and slide 2 an unreadable wall of legal text.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge body text on slides 7 to 11 and 14 to 23, and fix the margin line labels on slide 6 where they overlap the bars.

**Layout**

Two-column split slide: a black left column holds the title at top, a thin grey horizontal rule below it, and a bulleted list below the rule; the right column (starting at about 55% of slide width, slightly darker black with a faint left-edge gradient) holds a server photograph at top, a vertical stack of five partner logos at its right, two centred metric blocks in the middle, and a small italic footnote block at bottom right. The title and body are left-aligned at about 7.3% from the left edge; the title sits at the top margin around 7.7% from the top. The NVIDIA logo sits bottom-left at about 0.8% from the left edge and 94.7% from the top, with a green vertical bar at the far bottom-left corner. No slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

1) Photograph of an NVIDIA RTX PRO server (2U chassis) shown at an oblique three-quarter angle from above and to the left; two gold-coloured, finned, double-width GPU cards are seated at the top rear of the chassis, one near the centre-top and one at the right; the chassis body is dark grey metal with a silver-grey sheet-metal lid, cooling fans and vents visible toward the centre, and drive bays and hot-swap modules visible along the front-right edge. Lighting is soft studio light from above on a black background; composition is a centred, unboxed product shot with the object cropped to its own silhouette and placed on a black field. No overlay or tint. 2) Five partner logos in white on black, stacked vertically at the right: Cisco (wordmark with bar icon), Dell Technologies (wordmark), HPE (wordmark), Lenovo (white wordmark on a white rectangle, black text), Supermicro (wordmark in a white oval outline). 3) Three-metric block: '4X' FPS Real-Time Rendering, '6X' Throughput LLM Inference, '4X' Faster Synthetic Data gen, all in green large numerals with white captions, under the heading Multi Workload Acceleration with subheading vs. NVIDIA L40S. 4) Single metric: '45X' Performance Enterprise Workloads, under the heading Data Center Consolidation with subheading vs. CPU. 5) Horizontal grey rule across the left column at about 27.6% from top, from the left edge to about 47% of slide width. 6) Green vertical accent bar at bottom-left corner. 7) NVIDIA logo bottom-left (green and white). 8) A second NVIDIA logo image at bottom-right, which is not visibly rendered against the black background.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 background across the whole slide, with a slightly lighter black panel (#000000 with faint gradient edge) covering the right 45%. Pure black background throughout with white and NVIDIA-green (#76B900) accents and mid-grey (#8C8C8C) small footnote text. Title is set in a bold geometric sans-serif (NVIDIA Sans Bold style, close to Helvetica Bold), white #FFFFFF, roughly 4.4% of slide height per line with tight leading, about 3 lines. Bullet body text is NVIDIA Sans Regular, white #FFFFFF, about 2.2% of slide height, with green #76B900 round bullets; sub-bullets are about 2.0% in the same style. Metric numerals are NVIDIA Sans Bold in green #76B900 at about 4.1% of slide height; metric captions are regular white at about 1.5%. Section headings are NVIDIA Sans Medium white at about 2.7%. Footnote is italic grey #8C8C8C at about 1.1%. Shapes are flat with no shadows, gradients only as a subtle dark vignette on the right panel edge. The grey divider line is a thin light-grey stroke (about #A0A0A0, under 0.2% of slide height).

### Slide 23

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are consequential: the announcements section (14 to 23) uses very small body text, with slides 14 to 18 and 20 to 23 running in tiny type and slide 2 an unreadable wall of legal text.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge body text on slides 7 to 11 and 14 to 23, and fix the margin line labels on slide 6 where they overlap the bars.

**Text**

NVIDIA Blackwell-Powered Jetson
Thor Now Available, the Ultimate
Platform for Physical AI & Robotics
7.5x Orin
AI Compute
3.5x Orin
Energy Efficiency
3.1x Orin
CPU Performance
10x Orin
I/O Throughput
• These powerful new robotics computers are built for generative
reasoning and designed to power millions of robots across
diverse industries including manufacturing, logistics, agriculture,
transportation, healthcare, and retail
• With over 2,000 teraflops of AI compute, it unlocks new
capabilities such as simultaneous processing of multiple AI
workstreams and real-time inference.
• Agility Robotics, Amazon Robotics, Boston Dynamics, Caterpillar,
Figure, Hexagon, Medtronic and Meta are among the early Jetson
Thor adopters
• Also available is the new NVIDIA DRIVE AGX Thor developer kit for
safe, secure AV development
• Runs increasingly complex AV systems equipped with reasoning
vision language models, generative AI, and advanced sensor
technologies
• Leading automotive and AV companies are embracing Thor including
Aurora, BYD, Continental, Desay SV, Nuro, PlusAI, Volvo Cars, Waabi,
Wayve, and WeRide

**Layout**

Two-column split slide: a black left column (about 55% of the width) holds the title, a thin horizontal rule and a bulleted text block, left-aligned at about 7.3% from the left edge; a dark grey right panel (from 55.1% to the right edge) holds a 2x2 grid of feature cards above a product photograph. The title sits top-left at about 12.4% from the top, the rule sits at about 27.6% of the height, and the bullets start at about 34% of the height. The NVIDIA logo sits bottom-left at about 94.7% from the top, with no page number or footer text visible.

**Visuals**

Four outlined feature cards in a 2x2 grid on the dark right panel, each with a green line icon and two lines of white text. Top-left card: green neural-network node icon with text 7.5x Orin / AI Compute. Top-right card: green browser-window icon with a lightning bolt, text 3.5x Orin / Energy Efficiency. Bottom-left card: green square chip/grid icon, text 3.1x Orin / CPU Performance. Bottom-right card: green icon of a box with left and right arrows through it, text 10x Orin / I/O Throughput. Below the cards is a product photograph of the NVIDIA Jetson Thor developer module: a brushed silver, rectangular metal enclosure with a black perforated heatsink on its top, standing at a slight angle on a dark reflective surface, with a separate black circuit-board module tilted in front of it that carries a square chip with a green-tinted die and rows of components along its edge. The enclosure shows rear ports (USB, HDMI-type and Ethernet-type connectors) on its left end. Lighting is soft studio light from above, the background is near-black, and the scene is dominant in silver, black and grey with green-tinted chip highlights. The photograph is not cropped and is placed directly on the dark panel with no border.

**Design**

Background: Split background: solid black #000000 on the left about 55% of the slide, and dark charcoal #151617 (85% opacity) on the right panel, with the product photo and a dark studio floor texture over it. Black (#000000) left column and dark charcoal (#151617 at 85% opacity, appearing near #1B1C1E) right panel. Title text is white (#FFFFFF) NVIDIASans Bold sans-serif, about 4.0% of slide height, on three lines with tight leading. Body text is white (#FFFFFF) NVIDIASans Regular sans-serif, about 2.2% of slide height for first-level bullets and about 2.0% for second-level bullets. Bullet markers and accents are NVIDIA green #76B900. Card text is white NVIDIASans Regular about 1.7% of slide height. Card icons are drawn in #76B900 green line art. Cards have thin light grey outlines (about #6B6D70, roughly 1 px) with small corner radii (about 4 px) and no shadows. The horizontal rule is a thin light grey line (about #8A8C8E, about 1 px). A narrow green bar (#76B900) sits at the bottom-left edge beside the logo. No gradients are visible on the flat shapes.

### Slide 24

**Text**

Reconciliation of Non-GAAP to / GAAP Financial Measures

**Layout**

Full-bleed photographic section divider. Title block sits left-aligned in the upper-left third, starting about 4.3% from the left edge, with a thin vertical green bar flush against the left edge at the top. A NVIDIA logo image sits in the bottom-right corner, about 93% from the left and 94.7% from the top.

**Visuals**

Photograph-style abstract 3D render: dark black background at upper left with faint star-like specks, and sweeping glossy bright green curved ribbon or arch forms rising from the bottom and sweeping toward the upper right. Composition is diagonal, the green forms fill the lower-right two-thirds of the frame and are full-bleed, cropped at the bottom and right edges. Lighting is soft specular highlights along the edges of the green forms; dominant colours are NVIDIA green #76B900 and near-black #000000 with dark green shadows. No overlay other than the black base fill. Bottom-right image is a small NVIDIA logo (green eye mark with wordmark), placed as a logo, not a photograph.

**Design**

Background is near-black #000000 with full-bleed 3D green render over it. Title is white #FFFFFF, bold geometric sans-serif (NVIDIA Sans Bold), approximately 5.6% of slide height per line, two lines with tight leading (about 6% line pitch). Accent is NVIDIA green #76B900 for the vertical bar at the left edge. No shadows, no borders, no corner radii, no gradients on text.

### Slide 25

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: The reconciliation table lays out each non-GAAP adjustment in its own column with footnotes, so readers can trace GAAP to non-GAAP line by line.

**Text**

Reconciliation of Non-GAAP to GAAP Financial Measures / Non-GAAP / Acquisition-Related and Other Costs (A) / Stock-Based Compensation (B) / H20 related releases / Other (C) / Tax Impact of Adjustments / GAAP / Q2 FY26 / Gross margin ($ in million) / $33,960 / (49) / (58) / — / — / $33,853 / 72.7% / (0.1) / (0.2) / — / — / 72.4% / Gross margin excluding H20 related releases ($ in million) / $33,780 / (49) / (58) / 180 / — / — / $33,853 / 72.3% / (0.1) / (0.2) / 0.4 / — / — / 72.4% / Operating income ($ in million) / $30,165 / (86) / (1,624) / (15) / — / $28,440 / Net income ($ in million) / $25,783 / (86) / (1,624) / 2,231 / 118 / $26,422 / Shares used in diluted per share calculation (millions) / 24,532 / — / — / — / — / 24,532 / Diluted EPS / $1.05 / — / (0.06) / 0.09 / — / $1.08 / A. Consists of amortization of intangible assets, transaction costs, and certain compensation charges. / B. Stock-based compensation charge was allocated to cost of goods sold, research and development expense, and sales, general and administrative expense. / C. Other consists of legal settlement cost, net gain from non-marketable equity securities and publicly-held equity securities, net, and interest expense related to amortization of debt / discount.

**Layout**

A single full-width title sits centred at the top, above a large reconciliation table that spans nearly the full slide width with equal side margins of about 7 percent. The table has a grey header row with seven data columns plus a label column, a Q2 FY26 section label row, and six data rows of left-aligned labels and centred figures. Three footnotes sit left-aligned under the table, and the NVIDIA logo sits in the bottom-right corner. No slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Table with 8 columns and 9 rows. Columns: an unlabelled row-label column, Non-GAAP, Acquisition-Related and Other Costs (A), Stock-Based Compensation (B), H20 related releases, Other (C), Tax Impact of Adjustments, and GAAP. Rows: Q2 FY26 section label (no values); Gross margin ($ in million) with $33,960 / (49) / (58) / blank / — / — / $33,853, and its percentage row 72.7% / (0.1) / (0.2) / blank / — / — / 72.4%; Gross margin excluding H20 related releases ($ in million) with $33,780 / (49) / (58) / 180 / — / — / $33,853, and its percentage row 72.3% / (0.1) / (0.2) / 0.4 / — / — / 72.4%; Operating income ($ in million) with $30,165 / (86) / (1,624) / blank / (15) / — / $28,440; Net income ($ in million) with $25,783 / (86) / (1,624) / blank / 2,231 / 118 / $26,422; Shares used in diluted per share calculation (millions) with 24,532 / — / — / blank / — / — / 24,532; Diluted EPS with $1.05 / — / (0.06) / blank / 0.09 / — / $1.08. Negative values are in parentheses and dashes mark nil values. No highlighted points. Header row is filled with a mid-grey block and the body is black with thin grey rules. Also a small bright green vertical bar at the far right edge, bottom area, and the NVIDIA logo (green eye mark with white wordmark) in the bottom-right corner. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 (a full-bleed #76B900 green fill sits behind it and is fully covered). Solid black slide with a text-only corporate look. Title text is white bold geometric sans-serif (NVIDIA Sans Bold style) at about 4.4 percent of slide height, centred. Table header cells are filled #5E5E5E with white medium-weight sans-serif labels at about 2.2 percent of slide height; body text is white regular sans-serif at about 2.2 percent. Footnotes are italic sans-serif in grey #8C8C8C at about 1.2 percent of slide height. Accent is NVIDIA green #76B900 used only in the logo and the thin edge bar. Table rules are thin grey lines, square corners, no shadows or gradients.

### Slide 27

**Text**

Reconciliation of Non-GAAP to GAAP Financial Measures (contd.) / ($ in Millions) / Q3 FY26 Outlook / Non-GAAP gross margin / 73.5% / Impact of stock-based compensation expense, acquisition-related costs, and other costs / 0.2% / GAAP gross margin / 73.3% / Non-GAAP operating expenses / $4,200 / Stock-based compensation expense, acquisition-related costs, and other costs / (1,700) / GAAP operating expenses / $5,900

**Layout**

A centred title sits at the top of the slide (about 7 to 12 percent from the top) above a full-width financial table spanning about 6.9 to 93.0 percent of the slide width, from about 16.2 to 88.6 percent of the height. The table has a grey header row and two columns, a wide label column (left-aligned, indented for sub-items) and a narrower value column (centred) divided by a thin vertical rule at about 75 percent of the width. The NVIDIA logo sits bottom right at about 93 to 99 percent of the width, and there is no footer text or slide number.

**Visuals**

Table with two columns and a header row. Header row (grey #5E5E5E band, white text): column 1 '($ in Millions)', column 2 'Q3 FY26 Outlook'. Rows in order: 1) 'Non-GAAP gross margin' | 73.5%; 2) indented 'Impact of stock-based compensation expense, acquisition-related costs, and other costs' | 0.2%; 3) 'GAAP gross margin' | 73.3%; 4) an empty spacer row with no text, about 9 percent of the height; 5) 'Non-GAAP operating expenses' | $4,200; 6) indented 'Stock-based compensation expense, acquisition-related costs, and other costs' | (1,700); 7) 'GAAP operating expenses' | $5,900. Thin grey horizontal rules separate rows, and a thin grey vertical rule divides the columns. No chart, photograph or icon other than the NVIDIA logo at bottom right. No highlighted cells.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000. Black background (#000000) with white (#FFFFFF) title and table text and a grey (#5E5E5E) header band. A green (#76B900) accent bar runs along the bottom-right edge. Typeface is NVIDIA Sans (a geometric humanist sans-serif): Bold for the title, Medium for header labels, Regular for table body. Title is about 4.4 percent of slide height (cap height about 3.1 percent); body and table text is about 2.2 percent of slide height; header text about 2.5 percent. Lines are thin (about 1 px) mid-grey (#3A3A3A row rules, #5E5E5E column rule). No corner radii, shadows or gradients; shapes are sharp-cornered rectangles.

### Slide 28

**Text**

NVIDIA®

**Layout**

Single full-bleed image with no grid or columns. The NVIDIA logo sits in the top-left corner, about 2 percent in from the left and 4 percent down, and nothing else is set on the slide. No title, footer or slide number is present.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed 3D illustration of glossy green concentric arcs (rings) sweeping from the bottom-left corner up and across to the right, curving around a dark hollow opening at the lower right. The rings are lit from the upper left with specular highlights along their top edges, shaded to deeper green in the recesses, and the thin background lines and faint star specks fade into black at the top-left. A small NVIDIA logo (green eye-emblem square with white wordmark and ® mark) sits at the top-left. A second placed image lies at the bottom-right corner and is not visibly rendered on the page.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 with a full-bleed green-and-black 3D illustration covering the slide. Pure black field with a bright NVIDIA green (#76B900) 3D illustration and a white wordmark. No titles or body text. No typefaces are visible apart from the logo wordmark, which is a bold, wide, geometric sans-serif in white. The illustration has soft gradients and glossy highlights; no boxes, borders or corner radii.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- shape at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100 [#000000]: Full-slide solid black fill (Covers the whole slide; forms the visible black background.)
- illustration at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100 [#76B900]: Full-bleed 3D render of glossy green concentric arcs sweeping from bottom-left to right around a dark hollow, with a starfield at the top-left (Placed image 2880 by 1620 px covering the entire slide, not cropped or boxed. Arcs fade to black toward the top-left; ring highlights are pale green (#B5E61D approximately) on bright edges and the darkest recesses are near #0A1A00.)
- logo at x 2.2, y 3.8, w 23.3, h 14.3 [#76B900]: NVIDIA logo: green eye-emblem square with white NVIDIA wordmark and ® mark (Placed image 1863 by 641 px. Visible artwork spans about x 4.5 to 23.2 percent and y 6.9 to 14 percent, with transparent padding around it. Green emblem #76B900, wordmark white #FFFFFF, sitting directly on the black background and overlapping the starfield.)
